\f0\b \cf0 Caveats
\f1\b0 \
This is a 
\f0\b source census and batch plan
\f1\b0 , not VOICE-B1 extraction. Source rows are verified or located, assigned a preliminary status and batched for later extraction. No source row should yet be treated as an extracted finding, rubric recommendation or revised Voice / Singing rule.\
No current live Song / Voice / Singing output examples were supplied, so live-output QA remains pending.\
\pard\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf3 \strokec3 \
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 2. Source Census Scope\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 This source census covers public sources likely to support the Voice / Singing branch in four evidence families:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls1\ilvl0
\f0\b \cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	1	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 VOICE-B1 \'97 official admissions / schools / conservatoires / MT programmes
\f1\b0 \uc0\u8232 Real audition requirements, song tasks, accompaniment rules, cuts, self-tape requirements, acting-through-song, style expectations and live-room boundaries.\
\ls1\ilvl0
\f0\b \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	2	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 VOICE-B2 \'97 formal frameworks / exam boards / vocal descriptor sources
\f1\b0 \uc0\u8232 Vocal technique, musicality, pitch, timing, tone, diction, communication, style and musical interpretation descriptors.\
\ls1\ilvl0
\f0\b \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	3	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 VOICE-B3 \'97 professional casting / self-tape / recorded-audio process guidance
\f1\b0 \uc0\u8232 Audio/visual assessability, backing-track balance, self-tape burden, platform/process constraints, reader/accompanist/resource equity.\
\ls1\ilvl0
\f0\b \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	4	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 VOICE-B4 \'97 accessibility / fairness / vocal-health-safety / gap-fill sources
\f1\b0 \uc0\u8232 No-diagnosis rules, vocal strain/overload caution, speech/accent/access fairness, gender-diverse voice handling, disability/non-deficit overlays.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 This census does 
\f0\b not
\f1\b0  reopen Musical Theatre, Dance or Acting research, but it preserves their constraints: MT Vocal must remain visible where singing exists; Acting spoken-only speech must not be labelled as singing; Dance-only reports must not imply singing where none exists.\
\pard\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf3 \strokec3 \
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-S0 complete. Voice / Singing is ready for VOICE-B1 Targeted Batch Extraction.
\f1\b0 \
Rationale:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls2\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 The baseline and field-semantics problem is clear: 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  must be clarified as sung vocal for Song / MT while preserving Acting and Dance proxy protections.\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 A sufficient public source universe exists for official admissions, formal frameworks, self-tape/audio process and safety/accessibility overlays.\
\ls2\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 The strongest next evidence need is official admissions extraction, because it will establish real audition tasks, style categories, accompaniment rules, acting-through-song expectations and live-vs-recorded boundaries.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Critical blockers: none.\
Carry-forward limitations:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls3\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 No live Song / Voice outputs supplied.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Some source families are process-only and must not become scoring descriptors.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Formal frameworks must not become TapeCoach weights.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Vocal-health sources must shape diagnostic caution only.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Inclusive voice sources may be pedagogy/clinical-shaped and must be bounded carefully.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 14. Reusable Handoff Pack for VOICE-B1\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-S0 created a verified Voice / Singing source universe with 46 candidate source rows across four batches. The branch should begin with VOICE-B1, focusing on official admissions, schools, conservatoires and MT programmes. The most important baseline reality is that TapeCoach\'92s 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  field is overloaded: sung vocal for Song / Musical Theatre, speech delivery proxy for Acting / Monologue and movement-technique proxy for Dance. Song currently uses 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  as its central sung-vocal score; Musical Theatre uses both acting and vocal as central categories and remains the protected regression anchor. No score-field, weight, schema, backend, pipeline, cap, blocker, verdict or role-fit change is authorised.\
VOICE-B1 should extract real-world audition requirements from sources including ArtsEd, Mountview, Royal Academy of Music, GSA, Royal Welsh, RCS, USC, NYU Tisch, Carnegie Mellon, National Youth Choir, British Youth Music Theatre and related official audition pages. The extraction should prioritise song requirements, style/genre contrast, legit/contemporary/belt/mix/pop-rock language where source-supported, vocal range asks, accompaniment rules, backing-track/live-accompanist handling, song cut lengths, acting-through-song, lyric/storytelling language, self-tape audio/framing rules and live recall/redirection boundaries.\
VOICE-B2 will later extract formal descriptor language from LAMDA, Trinity, ABRSM, LCME and RSL/Rockschool. Those sources may inform observable vocabulary for pitch, time, tone, shape, performance, diction, style, musical communication and character/storytelling, but their marks and grade systems must not become TapeCoach weights. VOICE-B3 should handle professional process and self-tape/audio assessability. VOICE-B4 should handle vocal-health safety, no-diagnosis rules, accessibility and inclusive voice handling.\
Key risks to carry forward are VOICE-PRE-I01\'96VOICE-PRE-I13: technique over story, style not captured, vocal-health diagnosis risk, singing label leakage, acting-scene language leakage, implicit musical interpretation, repertoire overreach, fixed-material policy breach, production/audio polish as vocal merit, accent/diction/speech-difference bias, live-room stamina/direction overclaim, generic vocal praise and timestamp underproduction.\
The next run should be 
\f0\b VOICE-B1 Targeted Batch Extraction
\f1\b0 . It must not browse beyond the assigned B1 source pack once the pack is fixed, must assign final extraction status to every B1 source, and must preserve exact source IDs.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0
